logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE=""
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE=""
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+If you want to embed initramfs in the kernel, also pass ``CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE``. For example::
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+	make ARCH=openrisc CROSS_COMPILE="or1k-linux-" CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE="path/to/rootfs path/to/devnodes"
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+Your cpio archive should be specified in CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE and it
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE and directory and file names are not allowed in
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE.  Sources can be either directories or files -
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+and 'root-files' can be listed in CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE and a complete
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+entire CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE is passed to
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+usr/gen_initramfs.sh.  This means that CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+The config option CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE (in General Setup in menuconfig,
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE="../tb10x-rootfs.cpio"
logs/forensic-k3-k4-20260909113803/commit_f677e7377.txt:+CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE="$$KERNEL_INITRAMFS_SOURCE"
configs/airaos-x86_64.config:CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE=""
configs/airaos-x86_64-working.config:CONFIG_INITRAMFS_SOURCE=""
